    layer.querySelectorAll('[data-sensitive-modal-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=cancel);
    layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)cancel();};
    layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-modal-submit]').onclick=()=>{
      const current=layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-current]')?.value||'';
      const first=layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-first]')?.value||'';
      const second=layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-second]')?.value||'';
      if(!first)return error('비밀번호를 입력해 주세요.');
      if(mode==='set'&&first.length<4)return error('비밀번호는 4자 이상으로 설정해 주세요.');
      if(mode==='set'&&first!==second)return error('새 비밀번호 확인이 일치하지 않습니다.');
      closeSensitivePasswordModal({current,first});
    };
    const passwordInputs=[...layer.querySelectorAll('input[type="password"]')];
    passwordInputs.forEach(input=>{
      input.readOnly=false;input.disabled=false;input.inputMode='numeric';input.autocomplete='off';
      ['keydown','keypress','keyup','beforeinput','input','paste'].forEach(type=>input.addEventListener(type,event=>event.stopImmediatePropagation()));
    });
    setTimeout(()=>layer.querySelector('[data-sensitive-current],[data-sensitive-first]')?.focus(),0);
  });

function canConfigureSensitivePassword(){
  // __UEP_08160_SENSITIVE_CONFIG_ROLE__
  const role=String(currentRoleId?.()||'');
  return role==='admin'||role==='grade_head';
}
async function setSensitivePasswordFlow(){
  if(!canConfigureSensitivePassword())return toast('관리자·학년부장만 민감정보 비밀번호를 설정·변경할 수 있습니다.');
  const configured=sensitivePasswordConfigured();
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured});
  if(!result)return;
  if(configured&&await sensitiveHash(result.current)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash){
    return toast('현재 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
  }
  const passwordHash=await sensitiveHash(result.first);
  localStorage.setItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY,JSON.stringify({passwordHash,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}));
  lockSensitiveSession();
  toast('민감정보 열람 비밀번호를 저장했습니다.');
  if(state.activePage==='settings')render('settings');
}
async function unlockSensitiveInfo(){
  if(sensitiveSessionOpen())return true;
  if(!sensitivePasswordConfigured()){
    toast('설정 → 사용자·보안에서 먼저 비밀번호를 설정해 주세요.');
    return false;
  }
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'unlock',configured:true});
  if(!result)return false;
  if(await sensitiveHash(result.first)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash){
    toast('비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
    return false;
  }
  uepSensitiveUnlockedUntil=Date.now()+10*60*1000;
  return true;
}
window.addEventListener('beforeunload',lockSensitiveSession);
// 0.81.42: 재렌더링과 Electron 인라인 이벤트 제한에 영향받지 않는 보안 버튼 처리
document.addEventListener('click',async event=>{
  const sensitiveSet=event.target.closest?.('[data-sensitive-password-set]');
  if(sensitiveSet){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();await setSensitivePasswordFlow();return;}
  const sensitiveLock=event.target.closest?.('[data-sensitive-lock]');
  if(sensitiveLock){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();lockSensitiveSession();toast('민감정보를 잠갔습니다.');if(state.activePage==='settings')render('settings');return;}
  const subjectSet=event.target.closest?.('[data-subject-confidential-password-set],[data-uep134-pin-btn]');
  if(subjectSet&&!subjectSet.disabled){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();await setSubjectConfidentialPassword();return;}
  const subjectLock=event.target.closest?.('[data-subject-confidential-lock]');
  if(subjectLock&&!subjectLock.disabled){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();lockSubjectConfidential();render('settings');toast('과목별 신청현황을 잠갔습니다.');}
},true);




function updateStudentSignalIndicator(){
  const button=document.getElementById('studentSignalButton'); if(!button)return;
  const student=currentSignalStudent();
  if(!student){button.classList.add('hidden');button.classList.remove('signal-green','signal-yellow','signal-red');return;}
  const level=student.supportSignal?.level||'green';
  button.classList.remove('hidden','signal-green','signal-yellow','signal-red');button.classList.add(`signal-${level}`);
  const label=level==='red'?'확인 필요':(level==='yellow'?'지원 확인':'확인 완료');
  button.innerHTML=`<i></i><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(student.name||'')}</b><span>${label}</span>`;
  button.title=`${student.studentNo||''} ${student.name||''} · ${student.supportSignal?.summary||'별도 확인사항 없음'}`;
}

const UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY='uep_sensitive_security_v1';
let uepSensitiveUnlockedUntil=0;
async function sensitiveHash(value){
  const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(`UEP:SENSITIVE:${String(value||'')}`);
  const digest=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}
function sensitiveSecurityConfig(){
  try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY)||'{}')||{};}catch{return {};}
}
function sensitivePasswordConfigured(){return !!sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash;}
function sensitiveSessionOpen(){return Date.now()<uepSensitiveUnlockedUntil;}
function lockSensitiveSession(){uepSensitiveUnlockedUntil=0;}
function closeSensitivePasswordModal(result=null){
  const layer=document.getElementById('sensitivePasswordLayer');
  if(!layer)return;
  const resolver=layer._resolver;
  layer.remove();
  if(typeof resolver==='function')resolver(result);
}
function sensitivePasswordModal({mode='unlock',configured=sensitivePasswordConfigured()}={}){

      <div class="sensitive-password-form">
        ${change?'<label>현재 비밀번호<input type="password" autocomplete="current-password" data-sensitive-current></label>':''}
        <label>${mode==='unlock'?'비밀번호':'새 비밀번호'}<input type="password" autocomplete="${mode==='unlock'?'current-password':'new-password'}" data-sensitive-first></label>

    </div>`;

   });

function applyStudentFocusToPage(page){
  const student=studentFocusStudent(); if(!student)return;
  const classNo=String(student.className||classNumberOf(student)||'').replace(/반/g,'');
  if(page==='scores'){
    scoreMode='combined';
    const scope=$('#scoreScope'),cls=$('#scoreClass'),sel=$('#scoreStudent');
    if(scope)scope.value='individual'; if(cls){cls.value=classNo;cls.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const target=$('#scoreStudent');if(target){target.value=student.id;$('#scoreQueryButton')?.click();}});
  }else if(page==='admissions'){
    const scope=$('#admissionScope'),cls=$('#admissionClass'); if(scope)scope.value='individual';
    if(cls){cls.value=classNo;cls.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const target=$('#admissionStudent');if(target){target.value=student.id;$('#admissionQueryButton')?.click();}});
  }else if(page==='attendance'){
    attendanceStatStudentFilter=attendanceStudentKey(student);
  }else if(page==='records'){
    recordQueryMode='student'; recordClassNo=classNo||recordClassNo; recordStudentId=student.id;
  }
}
function goFromStudentDashboard(page,target=''){
  const student=studentFocusStudent();
  if(page==='scores')scoreMode='combined';
  if(page==='admissions')admissionMode=target==='combined'?'combined':(target||'combined');
  if(page==='attendance')attendanceMode=['official','late','night'].includes(target)?target:'statistics';
  closeDrawer();
  navigate(page);
  // navigate()는 좌측 메뉴 진입 규칙에 따라 생활기록부 첫 탭을 활동내역으로 초기화한다.
  // 학생 대시보드의 선택과목 전체보기에서는 해당 학생·교육과정 탭을 다시 명시하여 흐름을 보존한다.
  if(page==='records'){
    recordMode=target==='curriculum'?'curriculum':target==='final'?'final':'activities';
    recordQueryMode='student';
    if(student){recordStudentId=student.id;recordClassNo=recordStudentClass(student)||recordClassNo;}
    render('records');
  }
  if(page==='attendance'){
    attendanceMode=['official','late','night'].includes(target)?target:'statistics';
    if(student)attendanceStatStudentFilter=attendanceStudentKey(student);
    render('attendance');
    window.scrollTo(0,0);
  }
}

function navigate(page) {
  if(setupWizardActive){ renderSetupWizard(); return; }
  // v0.78.7: hidden legacy pages are routed into their integrated hubs.
  const routedTimetable=page==="timetable", routedDuties=page==="duties", routedForms=page==="forms";
  if(routedTimetable){ calendarHubMode="timetable"; page="calendar"; }
  if(routedDuties){ workEmbeddedMode="duties"; page="work"; }
  if(routedForms){ workEmbeddedMode="forms"; page="work"; }
  if(page==="alerts"){ outputHubMode="alerts"; page="outputs"; }
  if(page==="calendar" && state.activePage!=="calendar" && !routedTimetable) calendarHubMode="schedule";
  if(page==="work" && state.activePage!=="work" && !routedDuties && !routedForms) workEmbeddedMode="home";
  // 좌측 메뉴를 다시 누르거나 다른 메뉴로 이동하면 해당 모듈의 첫 화면부터 시작합니다.
  if(page==="inputs"){
    inputCenterConfig.manualText="";
  }else if(page==="outputs"){
    outputCenterState.home=true;
    outputCenterState.selected.clear();
    outputCenterState.reportScope="program";
  }else if(page==="records"){
    recordMode="activities";
    recordQueryMode="student";
    recordWriterArea="autonomy";
    recordWriterSelected.clear();
    recordSdgsDetail="";
  }else if(page==="scores"){
    if(typeof scoreQueryMode!=="undefined")scoreQueryMode="individual";
  }else if(page==="programs"){
    programMode="home";
    if(typeof programViewMode!=="undefined")programViewMode="list";
    if(typeof selectedProgramId!=="undefined")selectedProgramId="";
  }else if(page==="attendance"){
    if(typeof attendanceMode!=="undefined")attendanceMode="late";
  }else if(page==="students"){
    studentPageMode="list";
  }else if(page==="timetable"){
    if(typeof timetableMode!=="undefined")timetableMode="personal";
  }else if(page==="work"){
    workBoardMode="home";
  }
  if(studentFocusContext){
    const focusStudent=studentFocusStudent();
    const focusClass=String(focusStudent?.className||classNumberOf(focusStudent)||'').replace(/반/g,'');
    if(page==='scores') scoreMode='combined';
    if(page==='attendance'){attendanceMode='statistics';attendanceStatStudentFilter=focusStudent?attendanceStudentKey(focusStudent):'all';}
    if(page==='records'){recordQueryMode='student';recordClassNo=focusClass||recordClassNo;if(focusStudent)recordStudentId=focusStudent.id;}
  }
  state.activePage = page;
  save();
  $$("[data-page]").forEach((x) =>
    x.classList.toggle("active", x.dataset.page === page),
  );
  const info = {
    dashboard: ["오늘 먼저 확인할 항목", "업무 현황"],
    students: ["학생 기본정보 기준 원장", "학생정보"],
    dorm: ["학사생·외출 기록 통합 조회", "학사생정보"],
    timetable: ["선택과목 이동수업까지", "시간표"],
    attendance: ["정규·지각·야자 QR 통합", "출결"],
    scores: ["판단을 섞지 않은 원성적", "성적"],
    admissions: ["성적을 기준표로 가공", "입시"],
    records:

function submissionDateText(value){
  const raw=String(value??"").trim();
  if(!raw)return "-";
  const serial=Number(raw);
  let date=null;
  if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)date=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000);
  else{
    const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));
    if(Number.isFinite(parsed))date=new Date(parsed);
  }
  if(!date||Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))return raw;
  return `${date.getFullYear()}.${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}.${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
}
function reportSubmissionLabel(report){
  const date=submissionDateText(report?.finalSubmittedAt||report?.submittedAt);
  const count=Math.max(1,Number(report?.submissionCount)||1);
  return `${date} (${count}차 제출)`;
}

function selectionComparisonsForStudent(student){
  return (readonlyCache?.selectionComparisons||[]).filter(x=>(x.studentId&&String(x.studentId)===String(student?.id))||(x.studentNo&&String(x.studentNo)===String(student?.studentNo)));
}
function selectionComparisonMarkup(student){
  const rows=selectionComparisonsForStudent(student);
  if(!rows.length)return `<section class="selection-compare-panel"><header><div><small>PRE-CONSULTATION ↔ MAIN APPLICATION</small><h3>사전상담 ↔ 본신청 비교</h3></div><span>비교자료 없음</span></header><p class="selection-compare-help">기존 Google Form 사전상담과 리로스쿨 본신청 자료가 함께 연결되면 학기별 변경 내용을 표시합니다.</p></section>`;
  const changed=rows.filter(x=>x.status!=='일치').length;
  return `<section class="selection-compare-panel"><header><div><small>PRE-CONSULTATION ↔ MAIN APPLICATION</small><h3>사전상담 ↔ 본신청 비교</h3><p>학생이 상담 단계에서 생각했던 선택과 실제 본신청이 어떻게 달라졌는지 확인합니다.</p></div><span>${changed?`변경 ${changed}개 학기`:'전체 일치'}</span></header><div class="selection-compare-grid">${rows.sort((a,b)=>curriculumTermOrder(a.term)-curriculumTermOrder(b.term)).map(x=>`<article class="selection-compare-card ${x.status==='일치'?'same':'changed'}"><div class="selection-compare-title"><b>${escapeHtml(x.term)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(x.status)}</em></div><div class="selection-compare-columns"><div><small>사전상담</small><p>${x.preSubjects.map(s=>`<span>${escapeHtml(s)}</span>`).join('')||'<i>없음</i>'}</p></div><div><small>본신청</small><p>${x.mainSubjects.map(s=>`<span>${escapeHtml(s)}</span>`).join('')||'<i>없음</i>'}</p></div></div>${x.status!=='일치'?`<div class="selection-change-summary">${x.removed.length?`<span class="removed">빠짐 · ${escapeHtml(x.removed.join(' · '))}</span>`:''}${x.added.length?`<span class="added">추가 · ${escapeHtml(x.added.join(' · '))}</span>`:''}</div>`:'<div class="selection-change-summary"><span class="same">사전상담과 본신청이 같습니다.</span></div>'}</article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
}

function selectionErrorHistoryMarkup(student){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.selectionErrorTransitions||[]).filter(x=>String(x.studentId||'')===String(student?.id||'')||String(x.studentNo||'')===String(student?.studentNo||''));
  if(!rows.length)return `<section class="selection-error-history"><header><div><small>VALIDATION HISTORY</small><h3>사전신청 오류 이력</h3></div><span>특이사항 없음</span></header><p>사전 Google Form 신청 당시 발견된 문·이과 혼합·과학 위계·학기간 중복 오류 이력입니다.</p></section>`;
  const bad=rows.filter(x=>/지속|신규|재검토/.test(x.status)).length;
  return `<section class="selection-error-history"><header><div><small>VALIDATION HISTORY</small><h3>사전신청 오류 이력</h3><p>상담 당시 오류가 실제 본신청에서 해결되었는지 집중 확인합니다.</p></div><span>${bad?`확인 필요 ${bad}건`:'모두 해결'}</span></header><div class="selection-error-history-grid">${rows.map(x=>`<article class="${/해결/.test(x.status)?'resolved':/본신청 전/.test(x.status)?'waiting':'warning'}"><b>${escapeHtml(x.category)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(x.status)}</em><p>${escapeHtml(x.mainMessage||x.preMessage||'세부내용 확인')}</p></article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
}
function selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle){
  const rows=(bundle?.selected||[]).filter(x=>x.finalSubmittedAt||x.submittedAt||x.submissionCount||x.modifiedAfterNotice);
  const stamp=x=>{const raw=x.finalSubmittedAt||x.submittedAt||"";const serial=Number(raw);if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)return Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000;const parsed=Date.parse(String(raw).replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:0;};
  const row=rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>stamp(b)-stamp(a)||(Number(b.submissionCount)||0)-(Number(a.submissionCount)||0))[0]||{};
  const count=Math.max(1,...rows.map(x=>Number(x.submissionCount)||1));
  const rawDate=row.finalSubmittedAt||row.submittedAt||"";
  const parsedText=submissionDateText(rawDate);
  const y=(new Date()).getFullYear();
  const m=String(parsedText).match(/(\d{4})[.\/-](\d{1,2})[.\/-](\d{1,2})/);
  const afterNotice=Boolean(m && `${m[1]}-${String(m[2]).padStart(2,"0")}-${String(m[3]).padStart(2,"0")}`>`${y}-08-03`);
  return {date:parsedText,count,modifiedAfterNotice:afterNotice||rows.some(x=>Boolean(x.modifiedAfterNotice))};
}

=== app\resources\app\gyomuon.js :: curriculum @ 287806 ===
eText(value){
  const raw=String(value??"").trim();
  if(!raw)return "-";
  const serial=Number(raw);
  let date=null;
  if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)date=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000);
  else{
    const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));
    if(Number.isFinite(parsed))date=new Date(parsed);
  }
  if(!date||Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))return raw;
  return `${date.getFullYear()}.${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}.${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
}
function reportSubmissionLabel(report){
  const date=submissionDateText(report?.finalSubmittedAt||report?.submittedAt);
  const count=Math.max(1,Number(report?.submissionCount)||1);
  return `${date} (${count}차 제출)`;
}

}

function selectStudentDashboardById(studentId){
  studentSensitiveRevealKeys.clear();
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const key=String(studentId||'').trim();

  if(idx<0)return false;

  selectedStudentId=students[idx].id||students[idx].studentNo||"";

  return true;
}

   planBtn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();hideContent(true);setActive([planBtn,student,cross,subject].filter(Boolean),planBtn);},true);

   });

   });

  async function syncSchoolReadAuthBadge(){
    try{
      if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus)return;
      const status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:false});
      const authenticated=Boolean(status?.authenticated);
      const nodes=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,span,div,a')];
      for(const node of nodes){
        const text=String(node.textContent||'').trim();
        if(authenticated && text==='인증 필요'){
          node.dataset.uepSchoolReadAuthBadge='1';
          node.textContent='연결됨';
          node.title='UEP School Read API 인증 정상';
        }else if(!authenticated && node.dataset?.uepSchoolReadAuthBadge==='1' && text==='연결됨'){
          node.textContent='인증 필요';
          node.title='UEP 로그인이 필요합니다.';
        }
      }
    }catch(error){console.warn('[UEP] School Read auth badge sync failed',error);}
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{syncSchoolReadAuthBadge();setInterval(syncSchoolReadAuthBadge,5000);},{once:true});

  // Dashboard safeguard: do not leave a holiday-time mode visibly selected on an ordinary weekday.
  // This only normalizes the selector when both labels exist; it does not remove holiday support.
  function normalizeHolidayMode(){
    const nodes=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,[role="button"],.chip,.pill,.seg-btn')];
    const holiday=nodes.find(el=>/공휴s*시간|공휴일/.test((el.textContent||'').trim()));
    const regular=nodes.find(el=>/정규s*시간/.test((el.textContent||'').trim()));
    if(!holiday||!regular) return;
    const now=new Date();
    const day=now.getDay();
    if(day===0||day===6) return;
    const hs=getComputedStyle(holiday);
    const rs=getComputedStyle(regular);
    const holidayLooksActive=holiday.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true'||holiday.classList.contains('active')||holiday.classList.contains('selected')||hs.backgroundColor!==rs.backgroundColor;
    if(holidayLooksActive && typeof regular.click==='function') regular.click();
  }
  setTimeout(normalizeHolidayMode,300);
  setTimeout(normalizeHolidayMode,1200);
})();

function connectedNoticeVisibleToCurrentUser(item){
  if(!item)return false;
  const grade=String(item.targetGrade||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
  if(grade&&grade!=="1"&&!currentUserCanManageNotices())return false;
  const target=String(item.targetClass||"전체").trim();
  if(!target||target==="전체"||/전체|1~9|1-9/.test(target))return true;
  if(currentUserCanManageNotices())return true;
  const mine=currentTeacherHomeroomClass();
  if(!mine)return true;
  const mineNo=mine.split("-")[1];
  return target.split(/[,·\s]+/).some(v=>String(v).replace(/[^0-9]/g,"")===mineNo||String(v)===mine);
}
function directNoticeRows({activeOnly=true}={}){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const source=(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[]);
  // __UEP_08160_NOTICE_LATEST_REVISION__
  const revisionTime=item=>String(item?.modifiedAt||item?.updatedAt||item?.['수정일시']||item?.postDate||item?.date||'');
  const byId=new Map();
  source.forEach((item,index)=>{
    const id=String(item?.id||item?.noticeId||item?.['공지ID']||'').trim()||('__row_'+index);
    const prev=byId.get(id);
    if(!prev||revisionTime(item)>=revisionTime(prev))byId.set(id,item);
  });
  const connected=[...byId.values()].filter(connectedNoticeVisibleToCurrentUser);
  return connected.filter(item=>{
    if(item?.status==="삭제"||item?.status==="종료"||item?.done)return false;
    if(!activeOnly)return true;
    const start=String(item.postDate||item.date||"").slice(0,10), end=String(item.endDate||"").slice(0,10);
    if(start&&start>todayKey)return false;
    if(end&&end<todayKey)return false;
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b)=>Number(Boolean(b.important))-Number(Boolean(a.important))||String(b.postDate||b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.postDate||a.date||"")));
}

async function deleteOfficialAttendance(id="") {
  const key=String(id||"").trim();
  if(!key) throw new Error("삭제할 공결 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000"]);
  const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===key);
  if(pos<0) throw new Error("삭제할 공결 기록을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const sheetRow=pos+4;
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`);
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

function dormRulePriority(category=""){
  return ({exception:50,closure:40,exam:30,vacation:25,base:20,period:10})[String(category||"")]||5;
}

    String(payload.actualReentry||""),
    payload.priorNightAcademyHome?"Y":"N",
    payload.formRequired?"Y":"N",
    payload.individualOutingAllowed?"Y":"N"
  ]];
}

        const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),mo=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
        const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0

  const token=await getSheetsToken(account); const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'31_지각기록'!A3:P3000"]); const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[], rows=matrix.slice(1);
  let id=String(payload.id||"").trim(), sheetRow=-1;
  if(id){const pos=rows.findIndex(r=>String(r?.[0]||"").trim()===id); if(pos>=0)sheetRow=pos+4;}
  else {let max=0;rows.forEach(r=>{const mm=String(r?.[0]||"").match(/^LATE-2026-(\d+)$/);if(mm)max=Math.max(max,Number(mm[1]));});id=`LATE-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(5,"0")}`;}
  const values=lateAttendanceRow(payload,id);
  if(sheetRow>0)await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'31_지각기록'!A${sheetRow}:P${sheetRow}`,values); else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'31_지각기록'!A:P",values);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,id,data:await fetchLiveData({force:true})};
}
async function deleteLateAttendance(id=""){
  const key=String(id||"").trim(); if(!key)throw new Error("삭제할 지각 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath()); if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account); const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'31_지각기록'!A3:P3000"]); const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(r=>String(r?.[0]||"").trim()===key); if(pos<0)throw new Error("삭제할 지각 기록을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'31_지각기록'!A${pos+4}:P${pos+4}`);liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,data:await fetchLiveData({force:true})};
}

function dormRulePriority(category=""){
  return ({exception:50,closure:40,exam:30,vacation:25,base:20,period:10})[String(category||"")]||5;
}
function dormRuleRow(payload={},id,nowIso){
  return [[
    id,
    String(payload.category||""),
    String(payload.name||""),
    String(payload.start||""),
    String(payload.end||""),
    String(payload.days||"전체"),
    String(payload.effect||""),
    String(payload.entryTime||""),
    String(payload.exitTime||""),
    String(payload.lateEntry||""),
    String(payload.earlyExit||""),
    String(payload.reason||""),
    Number(payload.priority||dormRulePriority(payload.category)),
    payload.active===false?"N":"Y",
    String(payload.note||""),
    nowIso,
    String(payload.writer||"UEP"),
    String(payload.source||"UEP 규칙센터"),
    String(payload.operationState||""),
    String(payload.actualExit||""),
    String(payload.actualReentry||""),
    payload.priorNightAcademyHome?"Y":"N",
    payload.formRequired?"Y":"N",
    payload.individualOutingAllowed?"Y":"N"
  ]];
}
function dormRuleDateValue(value){
  if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return '';
  const raw=String(value).trim();
  if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){
    const serial=Number(raw);
    if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
      const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
      if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
        const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),mo=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
        const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
        return `${y}-${mo}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
      }
    }
  }
  const mm=raw.replace(' ','T').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return mm?`${mm[1]}-${String(mm[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(mm[3]).padStart(2,'0')}T${String(mm[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${mm[5]}`:raw;
}

    lateEntry:String(row[9]||'').trim(),earlyExit:String(row[10]||'').trim(),reason:String(row[11]||'').trim(),
    priority:Number(row[12]||dormRulePriority(row[1])),active:String(row[13]||'Y').trim().toUpperCase()!=='N',
    note:String(row[14]||'').trim(),modifiedAt:String(row[15]||'').trim(),writer:String(row[16]||'').trim(),source:String(row[17]||'').trim(),
    operationState:String(row[18]||'').trim(),actualExi

function dormRuleObjectFromRow(row=[]){
  return {
    id:String(row[0]||'').trim(),category:String(row[1]||'').trim(),name:String(row[2]||'').trim(),
    start:dormRuleDateValue(row[3]),end:dormRuleDateValue(row[4]),days:String(row[5]||'전체').trim(),
    effect:String(row[6]||'').trim(),entryTime:String(row[7]||'').trim(),exitTime:String(row[8]||'').trim(),
    lateEntry:String(row[9]||'').trim(),earlyExit:String(row[10]||'').trim(),reason:String(row[11]||'').trim(),
    priority:Number(row[12]||dormRulePriority(row[1])),active:String(row[13]||'Y').trim().toUpperCase()!=='N',

    operationState:String(row[18]||'').trim(),actualExit:dormRuleDateValue(row[19]),actualReentry:dormRuleDateValue(row[20]),
    priorNightAcademyHome:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[21]||'')),
    formRequired:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[22]||'')),
    individualOutingAllowed:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[23]||''))
  };
}
async function saveDormRule(payload={}){
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account), now=new Date().toISOString();
  const id=String(payload.id||`DORM-RULE-${crypto.randomUUID()}`).trim();
  const range="'40_학사외출규칙'!A3:X1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
  const row=dormRuleRow(payload,id,now);
  if(pos>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_학사외출규칙'!A${pos+4}:X${pos+4}`,row);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'40_학사외출규칙'!A:X",row);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const record={...payload,id,priority:Number(payload.priority||dormRulePriority(payload.category)),active:payload.active!==false,modifiedAt:now,writer:String(payload.writer||"UEP"),source:String(payload.source||"UEP 규칙센터")};
  return {ok:true,id,record};
}
async function deleteDormRule(id=""){
  const key=String(id||"").trim(); if(!key) throw new Error("삭제할 학사외출 규칙 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account), range="'40_학사외출규칙'!A3:X1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===key);
  if(pos<0) throw new Error("삭제할 학사외출 규칙을 시트에서 찾지 못했습니다.");
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_학사외출규칙'!A${pos+4}:X${pos+4}`);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  return {ok:true,id:key};
}

async function saveLunchDuty(payload={}) {
  const allowedRoles=new Set(['admin','grade_head','grade_manager','homeroom','subject']);
  if(payload.role&&!allowedRoles.has(String(payload.role))) throw new Error('급식지도 수정 권한이 없습니다.');
  const id=String(payload.id||'').trim();
  const date=String(payload.date||'').slice(0,10);
  const originalTeacher=String(payload.originalTeacher||payload.teacher||'').trim();
  const teacher=String(payload.teacher||'').trim();
  const dutyType=String(payload.dutyType||'급식실').trim();
  if(!teacher) throw new Error('변경할 급식지도 교사가 없습니다.');
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'42_급식지도계획'!A3:K1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const headers=(matrix[0]||[]).map(v=>String(v||'').trim());
  const idCol=headers.findIndex(h=>/기록ID|급식지도ID|^ID$/.test(h));
  const dateCol=headers.findIndex(h=>/운영일|일자|날짜/.test(h));
  const teacherCol=headers.findIndex(h=>/담당교사|지도교사|교사/.test(h));
  const typeCol=headers.findIndex(h=>/담당구분|구역|장소|역할/.test(h));
  if(teacherCol<0) throw new Error('42_급식지도계획의 담당교사 열을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>
    (id && idCol>=0 && String(row?.[idCol]||'').trim()===id) ||
    (!id && dateCol>=0 && String(row?.[dateCol]||'').slice(0,10)===date && String(row?.[teacherCol]||'').trim()===originalTeacher)
  );
  if(pos<0) throw new Error('수정할 급식지도 일정을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const sheetRow=pos+4;
  const colLetter=(index)=>{let n=index+1,out='';while(n){n--;out=String.fromCharCode(65+n%26)+out;n=Math.floor(n/26)}return out};
  await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'42_급식지도계획'!${colLetter(teacherCol)}${sheetRow}:${colLetter(teacherCol)}${sheetRow}`,[[teacher]]);
  if(typeCol>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'42_급식지도계획'!${colLetter(typeCol)}${sheetRow}:${colLetter(typeCol)}${sheetRow}`,[[dutyType]]);
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

    files.push({path:filePath,name:path.basename(filePath),extension:ext.replace(/^\./,''),size:stat.size,text});}catch{}}
  return {ok:true,files};
}

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}
function inputCenterProgramIdRaw(id=""){return String(id||"").replace(/^(common|selected)-/,'');}
function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}
async function classifyInputCenterBatch(payload={}){

    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');

    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
    let count=0;const storedType=inputCenterNatureToStoredType(payload.nature);const pid=String(payload.programId||'');const pk=String(payload.programKind||'');const note=pid?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${pid}`:'';const writes=[];
    for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(String(rows[i]?.[1]||'')===batchId){const sheetRow=i+4;const current=Array.from({length:24},(_,j)=>rows[i]?.[j]??'');current[3]=storedType;current[4]=payload.nature==='보고서'?'보고서 제출':payload.nature;let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(current[16]||''));}catch{}current[6]=inputCenterSemester(payload.programSemester||'',meta.submittedAt||'');current[17]='검수완료';current[19]=pid;current[20]=pk;current[23]=note||current[23]||'';writes.push({range:`'02_자료원본'!A${sheetRow}:X${sheetRow}`,values:[current]});count++;}
    await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,writes);
    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,count,batchId};
  }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}

async function readNoticeSheetMatrix(token, sheetName, range) {
  const result = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, [`'${sheetName}'!${range}`]);
  return result?.[0]?.values || [];
}
function noticePayloadRow(payload={}, id, nowIso, headerNames=[]) {
  const now = new Date(nowIso);
  const values={
    "공지ID":id,"학년도":String(payload.schoolYear||now.getFullYear()),"구분":String(payload.type||"공지"),
    "제목":String(payload.title||"").trim(),"내용":String(payload.content||"").trim(),
    "대상학년":String(payload.targetGrade||"1"),"대상반":String(payload.targetClass||"전체"),
    "게시일":String(payload.postDate||nowIso.slice(0,10)),"마감일시":String(payload.deadline||""),
    "확인필요":payload.confirmRequired===false?"N":"Y","제출필요":payload.submitRequired?"Y":"N",
    "담당부서":String(payload.department||"1학년부"),"담당자":String(payload.author||""),"상태":String(payload.status||"게시"),
    "수정일시":nowIso,"수정자":String(payload.author||"UEP"),"비고":String(payload.note||"UEP 화면 입력"),
    "공지성격":String(payload.noticeKind||payload.type||"일반공지"),"종례전달":payload.homeroomNotice?"Y":"N",
    "중요":payload.important?"Y":"N","상단알림":payload.topAlert?"Y":"N","게시종료일":String(payload.endDate||""),
    "마감유형":String(payload.deadlineType||"")
  };
  if(Array.isArray(headerNames)&&headerNames.length)return headerNames.map(name=>values[name]??"");
  return Object.values(values).slice(0,22);
}
ipcMain.handle("userAccount:update", async (_event, payload = {}) => {
  try {
    const account = await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
    if (!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
    const token = await getSheetsToken(account);
    const matrix = await readNoticeSheetMatrix(token, "01_사용자계정", "A1:Z500");
    const header = findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["사용자ID","이메일","성명","역할","사용여부"]);
    if(!header) throw new Error("01_사용자계정 헤더를 찾지 못했습니다.");
    const headers=header.headers, idx=name=>headers.indexOf(name), id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
    const rowIndex=matrix.findIndex((row,i)=>i>header.index&&String(row?.[idx("사용자ID")]||"").trim()===id);
    if(rowIndex<0) throw new Error("수정할 사용자를 찾지 못했습니다.");
    const updates={
      "역할":String(payload.role??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("역할")]??"").trim(),
      "관리자여부":payload.admin!=null?(payload.admin?"Y":"N"):String(matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("관리자여부")]||"N"),
      "담당학년":String(payload.grade??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("담당학년")]??"1").trim(),
      "담당반":String(payload.classNo??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("담당반")]??"").trim(),
      "조회범위":String(payload.viewScope??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("조회범위")]??"").trim(),
      "수정범위":String(payload.editScope??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("수정범위")]??"").trim(),
      "사용여부":payload.active!=null?(payload.active?"Y":"N"):String(matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("사용여부")]||"Y")
    };
    const row=Array.from({length:headers.length},(_,i)=>matrix[rowIndex]?.[i]??"");
    for (const [name,value] of Object.entries(updates)) { const col=idx(name); if(col>=0) row[col]=value; }

async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}

}
async function authorizeGoogleUser({loginHint=''}={}){
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  const verifier=crypto.randomBytes(48).toString('base64url');
  const challenge=crypto.createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest('base64url');
  const state=crypto.randomBytes(24).toString('base64url');
  let timer=null;
  let settled=false;
  let callbackResolve,callbackReject;
  const callbackPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{callbackResolve=resolve;callbackReject=reject;});
  const server=http.createServer((req,res)=>{
    try{
      const u=new URL(req.url||'/', 'http://127.0.0.1');
      if(u.pathname!=='/' && u.pathname!=='/oauth2callback'){res.writeHead(404);res.end('Not found');return;}
      const returnedState=String(u.searchParams.get('state')||'');
      const code=String(u.searchParams.get('code')||'');
      const error=String(u.searchParams.get('error')||'');
      res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':'text/html; charset=utf-8','cache-control':'no-store'});
      if(error||returnedState!==state||!code){
        res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>UEP Google 계정 연결이 취소되었거나 실패했습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP에서 다시 시도해 주세요.</p>');
        if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(googleOAuthError(error?`Google 승인 취소: ${error}`:'Google 승인 응답 검증에 실패했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_FAILED'));}
        return;
      }
      res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>Google 승인 정보가 UEP로 전달되었습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP로 돌아가 연결 완료 여부를 확인해 주세요.</p>');
      if(!settled){settled=true;callbackResolve(code);}
    }catch(error){if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(error);}}
  });
  try{
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{server.once('error',reject);server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',resolve);});
    const address=server.address();
    const port=typeof address==='object'&&address?address.port:0;
    if(!port) throw googleOAuthError('Google 승인용 로컬 포트를 열지 못했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_FAILED');
    const redirectUri=`http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
    const authUrl=new URL('https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('client_id',clientId);
    authUrl.searchParams.set('redirect_uri',redirectUri);
    authUrl.searchParams.set('response_type','code');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('scope','openid email https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets.readonly');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('code_challenge',challenge);
    authUrl.searchParams.set('code_challenge_method','S256');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('state',state);
    authUrl.searchParams.set('access_type','offline');
    authUrl.searchParams.set('prompt','consent');
    if(String(loginHint||'').includes('@')) authUrl.searchParams.set('login_hint',String(loginHint).trim());
    await shell.openExternal(authUrl.toString());
    timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(googleOAuthError('Google 계정 승인이 시간 초과되었습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TIMEOUT'));}},180000);
    const code=await callbackPromise;
    const body=new URLSearchParams();
    body.set('client_id',clientId);

    body.set('code',code);
    body.set('code_verifier',verifier);

nStampMs = value => {
    const raw=String(value||"").trim();
    const serial=Number(raw);
    if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)return Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000;
    const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));
    return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:0;
  };

    const stamp=String(row["타임스탬프"]||""); const prev=latestRawSelection.get(no);
    if(!prev || selectionStampMs(stamp)>=selectionStampMs(prev["타임스탬프"])) latestRawSelection.set(no,row);
    const meta=selectionSubmissionMeta.get(no)||{submissionCount:0,finalSubmittedAt:"",modifiedAfterNotice:false};
    meta.submissionCount+=1;
    if(selectionStampMs(stamp)>=selectionStampMs(meta.finalSubmittedAt))meta.finalSubmittedAt=stamp;
